# Codex-Optimized Code Review Question Bank: Corners Apart


## 01. Architecture and review discipline

### Q001: Live architecture map versus documentation

```text
Review the live Corners Apart Android repository and answer this focused question: Compare the repository's actual modules, packages, dependency directions, state owners, entry points, and runtime flows with PROJECT.md. Which documented architecture statements are stale, incomplete, or contradicted by code, and could any mismatch mislead future changes or reviews? Return a focused code-review answer with severity-ranked findings, concrete failure impact, and the smallest appropriate fix direction or test needed to verify the issue; do not make broad stylistic recommendations without a demonstrated consequence.

Important reliability instruction: AI reviewers can hallucinate. Inspect the live repository and all relevant tests and configuration before answering; do not treat `PROJECT.md`, comments, names, or memory as proof. For every repository-grounded claim, cite the exact file path and line range and explain the concrete execution path, invariant, reproduction, or test evidence that supports it. Classify each item as Confirmed defect, Confirmed missing coverage, Risk requiring validation, Manual verification required, External or policy confirmation required, or No issue found, using only the categories that actually apply. Do not invent files, APIs, test results, requirements, runtime behavior, measurements, or legal or policy conclusions. Report numerical impact only when it is measured with available artifacts or tooling; otherwise state what can and cannot be verified. Do not flag correct code as broken or propose a more complex design when the current simpler implementation is correct and sufficient; if no issue is found, say so plainly and explain why. Do not modify code unless this prompt explicitly asks for a patch.
```
